                Intent intent=new Intent(Intent.ACTION_CREATE_DOCUMENT).addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE)
                    .setType("application/zip").putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TITLE,suggestArchiveName(null));
                try{startActivityForResult(intent,REQUEST_BACKUP);}catch(Exception e){error("Záloha: "+e.getMessage());}
            });
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public void exportFolder(String folderId){
            if(folderId==null||!folderId.matches("[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,100}")){error("Neplatná složka");return;}
            runOnUiThread(()->{
                pendingArchiveFolderId=folderId;pendingArchiveSelectionIds=null;
                Intent intent=new Intent(Intent.ACTION_CREATE_DOCUMENT).addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE)
                    .setType("application/zip").putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TITLE,suggestArchiveName(folderId));
                try{startActivityForResult(intent,REQUEST_BACKUP);}
                catch(Exception e){pendingArchiveFolderId=null;error("Export složky: "+e.getMessage());}
            });
        }
    }
    private void closeUpload(){try{if(activeUpload!=null)activeUpload.close();}catch(Exception ignored){}activeUpload=null;activeUploadId=null;}
    private String readDb() throws IOException {
        synchronized(TrashPurger.LOCK){
        File db=new File(getFilesDir(),"projects.json"),backup=new File(getFilesDir(),"projects.bak");
        if(!db.exists()&&!backup.exists()&&!databaseReadFailed)return ""; // First installation only.
        try{String data=readValidJson(db);databaseReadFailed=false;return data;}
        catch(IOException primary){
            try{
                String data=readValidJson(backup);databaseReadFailed=false;
                android.util.Log.w("Zamerovac","Recovered last valid local backup",primary);
                return data;
            }catch(IOException secondary){
                databaseReadFailed=true;
                throw new IOException("Uložené zakázky nelze načíst; původní soubory zůstaly beze změny",primary);
            }
        }
        }
    }
    private String readValidJson(File file) throws IOException {
        try(InputStream in=new FileInputStream(file)){
            java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream o=new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();copy(in,o);
            String data=o.toString("UTF-8");
            try{JSONObject json=new JSONObject(data);if(json.optInt("schema")!=1||json.optJSONArray("records")==null)throw new IOException("Unsupported schema");}
            catch(JSONException e){throw new IOException("Invalid JSON",e);}
            return data;
        }
    }
    private void writeDb(String data) throws IOException {
        synchronized(TrashPurger.LOCK){
        if(databaseReadFailed)throw new IOException("Zápis zablokován: uložené zakázky nebyly bezpečně načteny");
        File next=new File(getFilesDir(),"projects.tmp"),dest=new File(getFilesDir(),"projects.json");
        File backup=new File(getFilesDir(),"projects.bak");
        try(FileOutputStream out=new FileOutputStream(next)){
            out.write(data.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));out.getFD().sync();
        }
        if(dest.isFile()){
            // Never replace the valid recovery copy with an unreadable primary.
            boolean validPrimary=true;
            try{readValidJson(dest);}
            catch(IOException invalid){validPrimary=false;android.util.Log.w("Zamerovac","Keeping existing recovery backup",invalid);}
            if(validPrimary)java.nio.file.Files.copy(dest.toPath(),backup.toPath(),java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        }
        try{java.nio.file.Files.move(next.toPath(),dest.toPath(),
                java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);}
        catch(java.nio.file.AtomicMoveNotSupportedException e){
            java.nio.file.Files.move(next.toPath(),dest.toPath(),java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        }
        }
    }
    private void syncCloud(){if(syncRunning){onSyncResult("Synchronizace již probíhá",null);return;}syncRunning=true;new Thread(()->{try{SyncEngine.Result result=new SyncEngine(this,settings).sync();onSyncResult(result.message,result.data);}catch(Exception e){error("Synchronizace: "+e.getMessage());}finally{syncRunning=false;}},"DriveSync").start();}
    private void scheduleSync(){try{JobScheduler jobs=(JobScheduler)getSystemService(JOB_SCHEDULER_SERVICE);JobInfo j=new JobInfo.Builder(14,new ComponentName(this,SyncJob.class)).setPeriodic(15*60*1000L).setRequiredNetworkType(settings.getBoolean("wifiOnly",false)?JobInfo.NETWORK_TYPE_UNMETERED:JobInfo.NETWORK_TYPE_ANY).setPersisted(true).build();if(settings.getString("treeUri",null)!=null&&settings.getBoolean("autoSync",true))jobs.schedule(j);else jobs.cancel(14);}catch(Exception e){android.util.Log.w("Zamerovac","Background job",e);}}
    @Override public void onResume(){super.onResume();if(settings!=null&&settings.getBoolean("autoSync",true)&&settings.getString("treeUri",null)!=null)handler.postDelayed(()->syncCloud(),1400);}
    @Override protected void onDestroy(){closeUpload();stopLaserConnection();videoThumbnails.close();clearCustomerExport();handler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);if(recorder!=null){recorder.release();recorder=null;}if(web!=null)web.destroy();super.onDestroy();}


    // The GLM 100-25 C is Bluetooth LE. Show ALL nearby LE advertisements (like
    // ImageMeter), preferring Bosch entries but not hiding unknown device names.
    private void stopLaserScan(){
        laserScanGeneration++; // Invalidate any delayed result from this scan.
        if(scanner!=null&&scanCallback!=null){
            try{scanner.stopScan(scanCallback);}catch(Exception ignored){}
            scanCallback=null;
        }
    }
    /** End an in-progress scan or live connection without forgetting the paired meter. */
    private void stopLaserConnection(){
        laserConnectionGeneration++;
        pendingLaserPermission=false;
        if(laserChooser!=null){laserChooser.dismiss();laserChooser=null;}
        stopLaserScan();
        activeLaserHistoryId="";
        boschStreamEnabled=false;
        boschCommand=null;
        if(classic!=null){
            try{classic.close();}catch(Exception ignored){}
            classic=null;
        }
        BluetoothGatt previous=gatt;
        gatt=null; // Late callbacks must not revive the connection in the UI.
        if(previous!=null){
            try{previous.disconnect();}catch(Exception ignored){}
            try{previous.close();}catch(Exception ignored){}
        }
        laserStatus("off","");
    }
    private void startScan(){startScan(false);}
    private void startScan(boolean showAllDevices){
        stopLaserConnection(); // Retire the previous session before any new callbacks arrive.
        String[] permissions=Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=31
            ?new String[]{Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN,Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT}
            :new String[]{Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION};
        boolean permitted=true;
        for(String permission:permissions)permitted &= checkSelfPermission(permission)==PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
        if(!permitted){
            pendingLaserPermission=true;pendingLaserShowAll=showAllDevices;
            requestPermissions(permissions,REQUEST_BLUETOOTH_PERMISSION);
            laserStatus("connecting","čekám na povolení Bluetooth");return;
        }
        final int connectionGeneration=laserConnectionGeneration;
        BluetoothManager manager=(BluetoothManager)getSystemService(BLUETOOTH_SERVICE);
        BluetoothAdapter adapter=manager==null?null:manager.getAdapter();
        if(adapter==null||!adapter.isEnabled()){
            error("Zapni Bluetooth telefonu a měřiče Bosch");
            laserStatus("off","");
            return;
        }
        stopLaserScan();
        final int scanGeneration=laserScanGeneration;
        scanner=adapter.getBluetoothLeScanner();
        if(scanner==null){error("Bluetooth LE není dostupné");laserStatus("off","");return;}
        scanned.clear();
        scannedNames.clear();
        laserStatus("connecting","vyhledávání zařízení");
        scanCallback=new ScanCallback(){
            @Override public void onScanResult(int type,ScanResult result){
                BluetoothDevice device=result.getDevice();
                if(device==null)return;
                String advertised=result.getScanRecord()==null?null:result.getScanRecord().getDeviceName();
                runOnUiThread(()->{
                    if(scanGeneration!=laserScanGeneration)return;
                    if(!scanned.contains(device))scanned.add(device);
                    if(advertised!=null&&!advertised.trim().isEmpty()){
                        String prior=scannedNames.get(device.getAddress());
                        if(prior==null||advertised.length()>prior.length())
                            scannedNames.put(device.getAddress(),advertised);
                    }
                });
            }
            @Override public void onBatchScanResults(java.util.List<ScanResult> results){
                for(ScanResult result:results)onScanResult(0,result);
            }
            @Override public void onScanFailed(int code){
                runOnUiThread(()->{if(scanGeneration!=laserScanGeneration)return;stopLaserScan();laserStatus("off","");error("Vyhledávání Bluetooth se nezdařilo ("+code+")");});
            }
        };
        try{
            scanner.startScan(null,new android.bluetooth.le.ScanSettings.Builder()
                .setScanMode(android.bluetooth.le.ScanSettings.SCAN_MODE_LOW_LATENCY).build(),scanCallback);
            handler.postDelayed(()->{
                if(scanCallback==null||scanGeneration!=laserScanGeneration)return; // scan cancelled or superseded
                stopLaserScan();
                ArrayList<BluetoothDevice> devices=new ArrayList<>(scanned);
                java.util.Set<String> leAddresses=new java.util.HashSet<>();
                for(BluetoothDevice found:scanned)leAddresses.add(found.getAddress());
                // Show paired Classic devices too, as ImageMeter does, but never
                // confuse a paired-only device with a current LE advertisement.
                try{
                    for(BluetoothDevice paired:adapter.getBondedDevices()){
                        if(!devices.contains(paired))devices.add(paired);
                    }
                }catch(Exception exception){android.util.Log.w("Zamerovac","Paired Bluetooth list",exception);}
                devices.sort((a,b)->{
                    String aa=String.valueOf(scannedNames.containsKey(a.getAddress())?scannedNames.get(a.getAddress()):a.getName()).toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
                    String bb=String.valueOf(scannedNames.containsKey(b.getAddress())?scannedNames.get(b.getAddress()):b.getName()).toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
                    boolean x=aa.contains("BOSCH")||aa.contains("GLM");
                    boolean y=bb.contains("BOSCH")||bb.contains("GLM");
                    return x==y?aa.compareTo(bb):(x?-1:1);
                });
                // Only start scanning when user explicitly requests it.
                String remembered=settings.getString("preferredLaserAddress","");
                if(!showAllDevices&&!remembered.isEmpty()){
                    for(BluetoothDevice device:devices){
                        if(remembered.equals(device.getAddress())){
                            if(leAddresses.contains(device.getAddress())){connectGatt(device);return;}
                            String name=String.valueOf(device.getName()).toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).replace(" ","");
                            if((name.contains("GLM50C")||name.contains("GLM100C"))&&!name.contains("100-25")){
                                connectClassicBosch(device);return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if(!showAllDevices){
                    devices.removeIf(device->{
                        String advertised=scannedNames.containsKey(device.getAddress())?
                            scannedNames.get(device.getAddress()):device.getName();
                        String name=String.valueOf(advertised).toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
                        return !(name.contains("BOSCH")||name.contains("GLM"));
                    });
                }
                if(devices.isEmpty()){
                    laserStatus("off","");
                    error(getString(R.string.bluetooth_laser_missing));
                    return;
                }
                String[] labels=new String[devices.size()];
                for(int i=0;i<devices.size();i++){
                    BluetoothDevice d=devices.get(i);
                    String name=scannedNames.containsKey(d.getAddress())?
                            scannedNames.get(d.getAddress()):d.getName();
                    labels[i]=(name==null||name.trim().isEmpty()?"Neznámé zařízení":name)
                            +" · "+d.getAddress()+(leAddresses.contains(d.getAddress())?"":" (spárované)");
                }
                laserStatus("off","");
                laserChooser=new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
                    .setTitle(showAllDevices?getString(R.string.bluetooth_choose_other):getString(R.string.bluetooth_choose_bosch))
                    .setItems(labels,(dialog,index)->{
                        if(connectionGeneration!=laserConnectionGeneration)return;
                        laserChooser=null;
                        BluetoothDevice d=devices.get(index);
                        if(leAddresses.contains(d.getAddress()))connectGatt(d);
                        else{
                            String n=String.valueOf(d.getName()).toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).replace(" ","");
                            if((n.contains("GLM50C")||n.contains("GLM100C"))&&!n.contains("100-25"))
                                connectClassicBosch(d);
                            else error("Zařízení je jen spárované. Zapni laser a zkus nové vyhledávání Bluetooth LE.");
                        }
                    })
                    .setNegativeButton("Zrušit",(dialog,index)->{if(connectionGeneration==laserConnectionGeneration)stopLaserConnection();})
                    .setOnCancelListener(dialog->{if(connectionGeneration==laserConnectionGeneration)stopLaserConnection();})
                    .show();
            },7000);
        }catch(Exception exception){
            stopLaserScan();
            laserStatus("off","");
            error("Bluetooth vyhledávání: "+exception.getMessage());
        }
    }
    // Backward-compatible Bosch Classic connection for older GLM 50C/100C.
    private void connectClassicBosch(BluetoothDevice device){
        try{
            if(gatt!=null){gatt.disconnect();gatt.close();gatt=null;}
            if(classic!=null)classic.close();
            String name=device.getName();
            activeLaserHistoryId=laserHistoryKey(device);
            final int generation=laserConnectionGeneration;
            classic=new BoschClassicReader(device,
                value->handler.post(()->{if(generation==laserConnectionGeneration)
                    js("window.onLaserValue("+JSONObject.quote(value)+","+JSONObject.quote("mm")+")");}),
                message->handler.post(()->{if(generation==laserConnectionGeneration)error(message);}),
                status->handler.post(()->{
                    if(generation!=laserConnectionGeneration)return;
                    if("connected".equals(status)||"receiving".equals(status))
                        settings.edit().putString("preferredLaserAddress",device.getAddress()).apply();
                    if("off".equals(status)){classic=null;activeLaserHistoryId="";}
                    laserStatus(status,name);
                }));
            laserStatus("connecting",name);
            classic.start();
            final BoschClassicReader pending=classic;
            handler.postDelayed(()->{
                if(generation==laserConnectionGeneration&&classic==pending&&!pending.isConnected()){
                    stopLaserConnection();error("Připojení Bosch Classic vypršelo. Zkus měřák znovu.");
                }
            },20000);
        }catch(Exception ex){stopLaserConnection();error("Bosch Classic: "+ex.getMessage());}
    }
    // Only invoked after the user has enabled the experiment AND tapped it.
    // The Bosch BLE command is not yet proven for GLM 100-25 C; never retry.
    private void sendExperimentalLaserTrigger(){
        if(!boschStreamEnabled||gatt==null||boschCommand==null){
            error("Dálková spoušť: nejdřív připoj laser Bosch");return;
        }
        if(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=31&&
           checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT)!=PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED){
            error("Dálková spoušť: chybí oprávnění Bluetooth");return;
        }
        int properties=boschCommand.getProperties();
        if((properties&(BluetoothGattCharacteristic.PROPERTY_WRITE|
                        BluetoothGattCharacteristic.PROPERTY_WRITE_NO_RESPONSE))==0){
            error("Dálková spoušť: laser nepodporuje zápis příkazů");return;
        }
        boschCommand.setWriteType(
            (properties&BluetoothGattCharacteristic.PROPERTY_WRITE)!=0?
            BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_DEFAULT:
            BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_NO_RESPONSE);
        boschCommand.setValue(BoschBleProtocol.REMOTE_TRIGGER);
        try{
            if(gatt.writeCharacteristic(boschCommand)){
                js("window.onExperimentalLaserTrigger?.('sent')");
            }else error("Dálková spoušť: příkaz se nepodařilo odeslat");
        }catch(Exception ex){error("Dálková spoušť: "+ex.getMessage());}
    }
    private void failGatt(BluetoothGatt connection,String name,String message){
        if(connection!=gatt)return;
        gatt=null;boschStreamEnabled=false;boschCommand=null;activeLaserHistoryId="";
        try{connection.disconnect();}catch(Exception ignored){}
        try{connection.close();}catch(Exception ignored){}
        laserStatus("off",name);error(message);
    }
    private void connectGatt(BluetoothDevice device){
        try{
            stopLaserScan();
            if(classic!=null){classic.close();classic=null;}
            if(gatt!=null){gatt.disconnect();gatt.close();gatt=null;}
            boschCommand=null;
            boschStreamEnabled=false;
            String name=scannedNames.containsKey(device.getAddress())?
                    scannedNames.get(device.getAddress()):device.getName();
            if(name==null||name.isEmpty())name="Bosch";
            final String deviceName=name;
            activeLaserHistoryId=laserHistoryKey(device);
            laserStatus("connecting",deviceName);
            final int generation=laserConnectionGeneration;
            gatt=device.connectGatt(this,false,new BluetoothGattCallback(){
                @Override public void onConnectionStateChange(BluetoothGatt connection,int status,int newState){
                    handler.post(()->{
                        try{
                            if(generation!=laserConnectionGeneration||connection!=gatt){connection.close();return;}
                            if(status!=BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Spojení Bluetooth selhalo ("+status+"). Zkus měřič znovu.");
                                return;
                            }
                            if(newState==android.bluetooth.BluetoothProfile.STATE_CONNECTED){
                                laserStatus("connecting",deviceName+" – hledám měření");
                                if(!connection.discoverServices()){
                                    failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Laser neumožnil načíst služby Bluetooth");
                                }
                            }else if(newState==android.bluetooth.BluetoothProfile.STATE_DISCONNECTED){
                                gatt=null;
                                boschStreamEnabled=false;boschCommand=null;activeLaserHistoryId="";
                                laserStatus("off",deviceName);
                                connection.close();
                            }
                        }catch(RuntimeException ex){failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Bluetooth: "+ex.getMessage());}
                    });
                }
                @Override public void onServicesDiscovered(BluetoothGatt connection,int status){
                    handler.post(()->{
                        try{
                            if(generation!=laserConnectionGeneration||connection!=gatt)return;
                            if(status!=BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Nepodařilo se načíst služby laseru");
                                return;
                            }
                            BluetoothGattService service=connection.getService(BoschBleProtocol.SERVICE_UUID);
                            BluetoothGattCharacteristic characteristic=service==null?null:
                                service.getCharacteristic(BoschBleProtocol.MEASUREMENT_UUID);
                            if(characteristic==null){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Zařízení nemá očekávaný protokol Bosch GLM LE. Vyber GLM 100-25 C.");
                                return;
                            }
                            boschCommand=characteristic;
                            int flags=characteristic.getProperties();
                            boolean indications=(flags&BluetoothGattCharacteristic.PROPERTY_INDICATE)!=0;
                            boolean notifications=(flags&BluetoothGattCharacteristic.PROPERTY_NOTIFY)!=0;
                            if(!indications&&!notifications||!connection.setCharacteristicNotification(characteristic,true)){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Nepodařilo se zapnout příjem měření");
                                return;
                            }
                            BluetoothGattDescriptor cccd=characteristic.getDescriptor(BoschBleProtocol.CCCD_UUID);
                            if(cccd==null){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Laser neobsahuje kanál pro potvrzení měření (CCCD)");
                                return;
                            }
                            cccd.setValue(indications?BluetoothGattDescriptor.ENABLE_INDICATION_VALUE:
                                BluetoothGattDescriptor.ENABLE_NOTIFICATION_VALUE);
                            if(!connection.writeDescriptor(cccd)){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Nepodařilo se povolit oznámení z laseru");
                            }
                        }catch(RuntimeException ex){failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Bluetooth: "+ex.getMessage());}
                    });
                }
                @Override public void onDescriptorWrite(BluetoothGatt connection,
                        BluetoothGattDescriptor descriptor,int status){
                    handler.post(()->{
                        try{
                            if(generation!=laserConnectionGeneration||connection!=gatt)return;
                            if(!BoschBleProtocol.CCCD_UUID.equals(descriptor.getUuid()))return;
                            if(status!=BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Laser nepotvrdil přihlášení k měření ("+status+")");
                                return;
                            }
                            BluetoothGattCharacteristic characteristic=boschCommand;
                            if(characteristic==null)return;
                            characteristic.setWriteType(
                                (characteristic.getProperties()&BluetoothGattCharacteristic.PROPERTY_WRITE)!=0?
                                    BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_DEFAULT:
                                    BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_NO_RESPONSE);
                            characteristic.setValue(BoschBleProtocol.START_STREAM);
                            if(!connection.writeCharacteristic(characteristic)){
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Nelze zapnout přenos Bosch měření");
                            }else if(characteristic.getWriteType()==BluetoothGattCharacteristic.WRITE_TYPE_NO_RESPONSE){
                                boschStreamEnabled=true;
                                settings.edit().putString("preferredLaserAddress",device.getAddress()).apply();
                                laserStatus("connected",deviceName);
                            }
                        }catch(RuntimeException ex){failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Bluetooth: "+ex.getMessage());}
                    });
                }
                @Override public void onCharacteristicWrite(BluetoothGatt connection,
                        BluetoothGattCharacteristic characteristic,int status){
                    handler.post(()->{
                        try{
                            if(generation!=laserConnectionGeneration||connection!=gatt)return;
                            if(!BoschBleProtocol.MEASUREMENT_UUID.equals(characteristic.getUuid()))return;
                            if(java.util.Arrays.equals(characteristic.getValue(),BoschBleProtocol.REMOTE_TRIGGER)){
                                if(status!=BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS)
                                    error("Laser nepotvrdil dálkovou spoušť ("+status+")");
                                return;
                            }
                            if(status==BluetoothGatt.GATT_SUCCESS){
                                boschStreamEnabled=true;
                                settings.edit().putString("preferredLaserAddress",device.getAddress()).apply();
                                laserStatus("connected",deviceName);
                            }else{
                                failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Laser odmítl aktivaci přenosu ("+status+")");
                            }
                        }catch(RuntimeException ex){failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Bluetooth: "+ex.getMessage());}
                    });
                }
                @Override public void onCharacteristicChanged(BluetoothGatt connection,
                        BluetoothGattCharacteristic characteristic){
                    final byte[] packet=characteristic.getValue()==null?null:characteristic.getValue().clone();
                    handler.post(()->{
                        try{
                            if(generation!=laserConnectionGeneration||connection!=gatt)return;
                            if(!BoschBleProtocol.MEASUREMENT_UUID.equals(characteristic.getUuid()))return;
                            String mm=BoschBleProtocol.millimetersText(packet);
                            if(mm==null)return;
                            boschStreamEnabled=true;
                            laserStatus("receiving",deviceName);
                            js("window.onLaserValue("+JSONObject.quote(mm)+","+JSONObject.quote("mm")+")");
                        }catch(RuntimeException ex){failGatt(connection,deviceName,"Bluetooth: "+ex.getMessage());}
                    });
                }
            },BluetoothDevice.TRANSPORT_LE);
            if(gatt==null)throw new IOException("Android neotevřel Bluetooth spojení");
            final BluetoothGatt pending=gatt;
            handler.postDelayed(()->{
                if(generation==laserConnectionGeneration&&gatt==pending&&!boschStreamEnabled)
                    failGatt(pending,deviceName,"Připojení laseru vypršelo. Zapni Bluetooth na měřáku a zkus to znovu.");
            },20000);
        }catch(Exception exception){
            stopLaserConnection();
            error("Připojení laseru: "+exception.getMessage());
        }
    }

}

===================== KONEC SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/MainActivity.java =====================


==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/MediaProvider.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;
import android.content.ContentProvider;
import android.content.ContentValues;
import android.database.Cursor;
import android.database.MatrixCursor;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.ParcelFileDescriptor;
import android.provider.OpenableColumns;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileNotFoundException;

/** Read-only provider for sharing user-imported media with Android viewers. */
public class MediaProvider extends ContentProvider {
    private String id(Uri uri){String s=uri.getLastPathSegment();return s!=null&&s.matches("[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{3,120}")?s:null;}
    @Override public boolean onCreate(){return true;}
    @Override public String getType(Uri uri){String id=id(uri);return id==null?null:getContext().getSharedPreferences("zamerovac",0).getString("mime-"+id,"application/octet-stream");}
    @Override public ParcelFileDescriptor openFile(Uri uri,String mode)throws FileNotFoundException{
        String id=id(uri);if(id==null||!"r".equals(mode))throw new FileNotFoundException();
        return ParcelFileDescriptor.open(new File(new File(getContext().getFilesDir(),"media"),id),ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_READ_ONLY);
    }
    @Override public Cursor query(Uri uri,String[] projection,String selection,String[] args,String order){
        String id=id(uri);if(id==null)return null;
        File f=new File(new File(getContext().getFilesDir(),"media"),id);
        String[] columns=projection==null?new String[]{OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME,OpenableColumns.SIZE}:projection;
        MatrixCursor c=new MatrixCursor(columns);Object[] row=new Object[columns.length];
        String name=getContext().getSharedPreferences("zamerovac",0).getString("name-"+id,id);
        for(int i=0;i<columns.length;i++){if(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME.equals(columns[i]))row[i]=name;else if(OpenableColumns.SIZE.equals(columns[i]))row[i]=f.length();}
        c.addRow(row);return c;
    }
    @Override public Uri insert(Uri u,ContentValues v){throw new UnsupportedOperationException();}
    @Override public int delete(Uri u,String s,String[] a){throw new UnsupportedOperationException();}
    @Override public int update(Uri u,ContentValues v,String s,String[] a){throw new UnsupportedOperationException();}
}

===================== KONEC SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/MediaProvider.java =====================


==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/PhotoAddressResolver.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;

import android.Manifest;
import android.app.Activity;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.location.Address;
import android.location.Geocoder;
import android.location.Location;
import android.location.LocationManager;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Optional, on-demand address lookup for a photograph captured by the user.
 * No continuous tracking; coordinates never leave this class or get stored
 * in a record. Geocoding may need an Internet connection/provider.
 */
public final class PhotoAddressResolver {
    private PhotoAddressResolver(){}

    public static void resolve(Activity activity,Consumer<String> result){
        boolean precise=activity.checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)==PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
        boolean approximate=activity.checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION)==PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
        if(!precise&&!approximate){result.accept("");return;}
        LocationManager lm=(LocationManager)activity.getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
        if(lm==null){result.accept("");return;}
        String provider=precise?LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER:LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER;
        try{
            if(!lm.isProviderEnabled(provider)){
                if(approximate&&lm.isProviderEnabled(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER))provider=LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER;
                else {result.accept("");return;}
            }
            AtomicBoolean finished=new AtomicBoolean(false);
            Handler main=new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
            Runnable fallback=()->{
                if(finished.compareAndSet(false,true)){
                    Location gps=null,network=null;
                    try{
                        if(precise)gps=lm.getLastKnownLocation(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER);
                        network=lm.getLastKnownLocation(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER);
                    }catch(SecurityException ignored){}
                    // Fallback if there was no fresh GPS fix (common indoors).
                    Location best=isFresh(gps)?gps:null;
                    if(isFresh(network)&&(best==null||
                        network.getTime()>best.getTime()+30000L))best=network;
                    if(best!=null)geocode(activity,best,result);
                    else result.accept("");
                }
            };
            main.postDelayed(fallback,14000);
            Consumer<Location> location=loc->{
                // A null GPS result must NOT complete the lookup: the
                // network provider may still return a usable indoor fix.
                if(!isFresh(loc))return;
                if(!finished.compareAndSet(false,true))return;
                main.removeCallbacks(fallback);
                geocode(activity,loc,result);
            };
            // Indoors GPS may never lock; after four seconds also try the
            // network/Wi-Fi provider. Whichever returns a fresh fix first wins.
            if(precise&&lm.isProviderEnabled(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER)){
                main.postDelayed(()->{
                    if(finished.get())return;
                    try{
                        if(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=30){
                            lm.getCurrentLocation(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER,
                                null,activity.getMainExecutor(),location::accept);
                        }else{
                            lm.requestSingleUpdate(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER,
                                new android.location.LocationListener(){
                                  @Override public void onLocationChanged(Location l){location.accept(l);}
                                  @Override public void onStatusChanged(String p,int status,android.os.Bundle extras){}
                                  @Override public void onProviderEnabled(String p){}
                                  @Override public void onProviderDisabled(String p){}
                                },Looper.getMainLooper());
                        }
                    }catch(Exception ignored){}
                },4000);
            }
            if(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=30){
                lm.getCurrentLocation(provider,null,activity.getMainExecutor(),location::accept);
            }else{
                lm.requestSingleUpdate(provider,new android.location.LocationListener(){
                    @Override public void onLocationChanged(Location newLocation){location.accept(newLocation);}
                    @Override public void onStatusChanged(String p,int status,android.os.Bundle extras){}
                    @Override public void onProviderEnabled(String p){}
                    @Override public void onProviderDisabled(String p){}
                },Looper.getMainLooper());
            }
        }catch(Exception e){result.accept("");}
    }
    private static boolean isFresh(Location loc){
        return loc!=null&&loc.getTime()>0&&
          Math.abs(System.currentTimeMillis()-loc.getTime())<5*60*1000L;
    }
    private static void geocode(Activity activity,Location loc,Consumer<String> result){
        new Thread(()->{
            String value="";
            try{
                if(Geocoder.isPresent()){
                    Geocoder coder=new Geocoder(activity,Locale.forLanguageTag("cs-CZ"));
                    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
                    List<Address> found=coder.getFromLocation(loc.getLatitude(),loc.getLongitude(),1);
                    if(found!=null&&!found.isEmpty()){
                        Address a=found.get(0);
                        // Human-readable address only (no latitude/longitude).
                        String street=a.getThoroughfare(),number=a.getSubThoroughfare();
                        String city=a.getLocality();
                        if(city==null||city.isEmpty())city=a.getSubAdminArea();
                        String postcode=a.getPostalCode();
                        // Menší obce nemají pojmenované ulice: getThoroughfare()
                        // bývá null, ale samotné číslo domu může být k dispozici.
                        if(number==null||number.trim().isEmpty()){
                            String feature=a.getFeatureName();
                            if(feature!=null&&feature.matches("[0-9]{1,5}(?:/[0-9]{1,5})?"))
                                number=feature;
                        }
                        StringBuilder sb=new StringBuilder();
                        if(street!=null&&!street.trim().isEmpty())sb.append(street.trim());
                        else if(number!=null&&!number.trim().isEmpty()&&city!=null)
                            sb.append(city.trim());
                        if(number!=null&&!number.trim().isEmpty()){
                            if(sb.length()>0)sb.append(" ");
                            sb.append(number.trim());
                        }
                        if(postcode!=null&&!postcode.isEmpty()||city!=null&&!city.isEmpty()){
                            if(sb.length()>0)sb.append(", ");
                            if(postcode!=null&&!postcode.isEmpty())sb.append(postcode).append(" ");
                            if(city!=null)sb.append(city);
                        }
                        value=sb.toString().trim();
                        if(value.isEmpty()&&a.getMaxAddressLineIndex()>=0)
                            value=a.getAddressLine(0);
                    }
                }
            }catch(Exception ignored){}
            final String address=value;
            activity.runOnUiThread(()->result.accept(address));
        },"Photo-address-geocoder").start();
    }
}

===================== KONEC SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/PhotoAddressResolver.java =====================


==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/RasterPdfWriter.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;

import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.*;

/** Flattened RGB pages only: no metadata, attachments or editable project objects.
 * Writes one page at a time so a long note does not retain all image bytes in RAM. */
final class RasterPdfWriter {
    private RasterPdfWriter() {}
    static void write(OutputStream out,int width,int height,byte[] jpeg)throws IOException {
        Document document=new Document(out);document.add(width,height,jpeg);document.finish();
    }
    static final class Document {
        private final OutputStream out;
        private long position;
        private final ArrayList<Long> offsets=new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList(0L,0L,0L));
        private int pages;
        private boolean finished;
        Document(OutputStream out)throws IOException {this.out=out;text("%PDF-1.4\n%âãÏÓ\n");}
        void add(int width,int height,byte[] jpeg)throws IOException {
            if(finished||pages>=200||width<1||height<1||jpeg==null||jpeg.length<4||
                    jpeg[0]!=(byte)0xff||jpeg[1]!=(byte)0xd8)throw new IOException("Neplatný obrázek PDF");
            int page=3+pages*3,image=page+1,content=page+2;
            object(page,"<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 "+width+" "+height+"] /Resources << /XObject << /Im0 "+image+" 0 R >> >> /Contents "+content+" 0 R >>");
            start(image);text("<< /Type /XObject /Subtype /Image /Width "+width+" /Height "+height+
                " /ColorSpace /DeviceRGB /BitsPerComponent 8 /Filter /DCTDecode /Length "+jpeg.length+" >>\nstream\n");
            bytes(jpeg);text("\nendstream\nendobj\n");
            String draw="q\n"+width+" 0 0 "+height+" 0 0 cm\n/Im0 Do\nQ\n";
            start(content);text("<< /Length "+draw.getBytes(StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1).length+" >>\nstream\n"+draw+"endstream\nendobj\n");
            pages++;
        }
        void finish()throws IOException {
            if(finished||pages==0)throw new IOException("Prázdné nebo uzavřené PDF");
            object(1,"<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>");
            StringBuilder kids=new StringBuilder();for(int i=0;i<pages;i++)kids.append(3+i*3).append(" 0 R ");
            object(2,"<< /Type /Pages /Kids ["+kids+"] /Count "+pages+" >>");
            long xref=position;int count=offsets.size();
            text("xref\n0 "+count+"\n0000000000 65535 f \n");
            for(int i=1;i<count;i++)text(String.format(Locale.ROOT,"%010d 00000 n \n",offsets.get(i)));
            text("trailer\n<< /Size "+count+" /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n"+xref+"\n%%EOF\n");
            out.flush();finished=true;
        }
        private void start(int id)throws IOException {
            while(offsets.size()<=id)offsets.add(0L);offsets.set(id,position);text(id+" 0 obj\n");
        }
        private void object(int id,String body)throws IOException {start(id);text(body+"\nendobj\n");}
        private void text(String value)throws IOException {bytes(value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1));}
        private void bytes(byte[] value)throws IOException {out.write(value);position+=value.length;}
    }
}

===================== KONEC SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/RasterPdfWriter.java =====================


==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/SyncEngine.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;

import android.content.ContentResolver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.database.Cursor;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.provider.DocumentsContract;
import android.provider.DocumentsContract.Document;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

/** Local-first, record-wise cloud sync using Android's Storage Access Framework.
 * The storage provider is user-selected; Google Drive is supported when its
 * DocumentsProvider offers a writable folder. No Google account secrets in app.
 */
public class SyncEngine {
    public static final class Result {
        public final String message;
        public final JSONObject data;
        Result(String msg, JSONObject json) { message=msg;data=json; }
    }
    private final Context context;
    private final ContentResolver resolver;
    private final SharedPreferences prefs;
    private final File home, mediaDir;
    private final Uri tree;

    public SyncEngine(Context c, SharedPreferences p) {
        context=c.getApplicationContext(); resolver=context.getContentResolver(); prefs=p;
        home=context.getFilesDir();mediaDir=new File(home,"media");
        mediaDir.mkdirs();String s=p.getString("treeUri",null); tree=s==null?null:Uri.parse(s);
    }
    private static String slurp(InputStream in) throws Exception {
        ByteArrayOutputStream b=new ByteArrayOutputStream();byte[] block=new byte[65536];int n;
        while((n=in.read(block))!=-1)b.write(block,0,n);
        return b.toString("UTF-8");
    }
    private String loadLocal() throws Exception {
        File f=new File(home,"projects.json");if(!f.isFile())return "";
        try(InputStream in=new FileInputStream(f)){return slurp(in);}
    }
    private void saveLocal(String text) throws Exception {
        File dest=new File(home,"projects.json"), tmp=new File(home,"projects.sync.tmp");
        try(FileOutputStream out=new FileOutputStream(tmp)){out.write(text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));out.getFD().sync();}
        if(!tmp.renameTo(dest))throw new Exception("Nelze uložit lokální databázi");
    }
    private JSONObject parse(String text) throws Exception {
        if(text==null||text.isEmpty())return new JSONObject().put("schema",1).put("records",new JSONArray()).put("prefs",new JSONObject());
        JSONObject o=new JSONObject(text);if(o.optInt("schema")!=1)throw new Exception("Nepodporovaná verze souboru zakázek");
        return o;
    }
    private Map<String,String> baseline(){Map<String,String> m=new HashMap<>();try{
        JSONObject j=new JSONObject(prefs.getString("syncBase","{}"));for(java.util.Iterator<String> it=j.keys();it.hasNext();){String k=it.next();m.put(k,j.optString(k));}
        }catch(Exception ignored){}return m;}
    private String nextBaseline="{}";
    private JSONObject merge(JSONObject local, JSONObject remote) throws Exception {
        Map<String,JSONObject> l=new HashMap<>(), r=new HashMap<>();
        JSONArray a=local.optJSONArray("records"), b=remote.optJSONArray("records");
        if(a!=null)for(int i=0;i<a.length();i++){JSONObject rec=a.optJSONObject(i);if(rec!=null&&rec.has("id"))l.put(rec.getString("id"),rec);}
        if(b!=null)for(int i=0;i<b.length();i++){JSONObject rec=b.optJSONObject(i);if(rec!=null&&rec.has("id"))r.put(rec.getString("id"),rec);}
        Set<String> purged=TrashPurger.purgedIds(local);purged.addAll(TrashPurger.purgedIds(remote));
        Map<String,String> base=baseline();Set<String> all=new HashSet<>();all.addAll(l.keySet());all.addAll(r.keySet());
        JSONArray merged=new JSONArray();JSONObject nextBase=new JSONObject();
        // Deterministic order keeps generated JSON stable.
        ArrayList<String> sorted=new ArrayList<>(all);java.util.Collections.sort(sorted);
        for(String id: sorted){JSONObject lv=l.get(id),rv=r.get(id), chosen;
            if(purged.contains(id)){
                if(lv!=null&&lv.optBoolean("deleted"))lv=null;
                if(rv!=null&&rv.optBoolean("deleted"))rv=null;
                if(lv==null&&rv==null)continue;
            }
            if(lv==null)chosen=rv;
            else if(rv==null)chosen=lv;
            else {
                String lrev=lv.optString("rev",""),rrev=rv.optString("rev","");
                String known=base.get(id);
                if(lrev.equals(rrev))chosen=lv;
                else if(known!=null&&known.equals(lrev))chosen=rv;
                else if(known!=null&&known.equals(rrev))chosen=lv;
                else {
                    // Preserve independent edits; do not discard either side.
                    boolean localNewer=lv.optString("updatedAt","").compareTo(rv.optString("updatedAt",""))>=0;
                    chosen=localNewer?lv:rv;
                    JSONObject losing=new JSONObject((localNewer?rv:lv).toString());
                    losing.put("id",UUID.randomUUID().toString());losing.put("title",losing.optString("title","Položka")+" (konflikt synchronizace)");
                    losing.put("rev",UUID.randomUUID().toString());losing.put("conflict",true);
                    merged.put(losing);
                }
            }
            merged.put(chosen);nextBase.put(id,chosen.optString("rev",""));
        }
        JSONObject result=new JSONObject(local.toString());result.put("records",merged);result.put("trashPurgedIds",new JSONArray(purged));
        // Preferences are device-local (screen size, tool order, colors). Never overwrite.
        nextBaseline=nextBase.toString();
        return result;
    }
    private Uri dir(String parentId,String name,boolean create) throws Exception{
        Uri parent=DocumentsContract.buildChildDocumentsUriUsingTree(tree,parentId);
        try(Cursor c=resolver.query(parent,new String[]{Document.COLUMN_DOCUMENT_ID,Document.COLUMN_DISPLAY_NAME,Document.COLUMN_MIME_TYPE},null,null,null)){
            if(c!=null)while(c.moveToNext())if(name.equals(c.getString(1))&&Document.MIME_TYPE_DIR.equals(c.getString(2))){
                return DocumentsContract.buildDocumentUriUsingTree(tree,c.getString(0));}
        }
        if(!create)return null;
        return DocumentsContract.createDocument(resolver,DocumentsContract.buildDocumentUriUsingTree(tree,parentId),Document.MIME_TYPE_DIR,name);
    }
    private static class Entry {Uri uri;String mime;Entry(Uri u,String m){uri=u;mime=m;}}
    private Map<String,Entry> children(String parent) throws Exception{
        Map<String,Entry> result=new HashMap<>();Uri query=DocumentsContract.buildChildDocumentsUriUsingTree(tree,parent);
        try(Cursor c=resolver.query(query,new String[]{Document.COLUMN_DOCUMENT_ID,Document.COLUMN_DISPLAY_NAME,Document.COLUMN_MIME_TYPE},null,null,null)){
            if(c!=null)while(c.moveToNext())result.put(c.getString(1),new Entry(DocumentsContract.buildDocumentUriUsingTree(tree,c.getString(0)),c.getString(2)));
        }
        return result;
    }
    private String readDoc(Uri doc) throws Exception{try(InputStream in=resolver.openInputStream(doc)){if(in==null)throw new Exception("Nelze číst cloudový soubor");return slurp(in);}}
    private void writeDoc(Uri doc,String text) throws Exception{try(OutputStream out=resolver.openOutputStream(doc,"wt")){if(out==null)throw new Exception("Nelze zapisovat na disk");out.write(text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));out.flush();}}
    private String docId(Uri uri){return DocumentsContract.getDocumentId(uri);}
    private String contentType(String id){return prefs.getString("mime-"+id,"application/octet-stream");}
    private void copy(InputStream in,OutputStream out) throws Exception{byte[] buf=new byte[65536];int n;while((n=in.read(buf))!=-1)out.write(buf,0,n);}
    private void syncMedia(Uri mediaFolder) throws Exception {
        Map<String,Entry> remote=children(docId(mediaFolder));
        for(File f:mediaDir.listFiles()==null?new File[0]:mediaDir.listFiles()){
            if(!f.isFile()||!f.getName().matches("[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{3,120}"))continue;
            if(!remote.containsKey(f.getName())){
                Uri created=DocumentsContract.createDocument(resolver,mediaFolder,contentType(f.getName()),f.getName());
                if(created==null)throw new Exception("Nelze vytvořit cloudový soubor "+f.getName());
                try(InputStream in=new FileInputStream(f);OutputStream out=resolver.openOutputStream(created,"w")){if(out==null)throw new Exception("Cloudový zápis odmítnut");copy(in,out);}
            }
        }
        for(Map.Entry<String,Entry> it:remote.entrySet()){
            String id=it.getKey();if(!id.matches("[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{3,120}"))continue;
            File f=new File(mediaDir,id);
            if(!f.exists()){
                File tmp=new File(mediaDir,id+".tmp");
                try(InputStream in=resolver.openInputStream(it.getValue().uri);OutputStream out=new FileOutputStream(tmp)){if(in==null)throw new Exception("Chybí příloha "+id);copy(in,out);}
                if(!tmp.renameTo(f))throw new Exception("Nelze uložit přílohu "+id);
            }
            prefs.edit().putString("mime-"+id,it.getValue().mime).apply();
        }
    }
    public synchronized Result sync() throws Exception{
        synchronized(TrashPurger.LOCK){
        if(tree==null)throw new Exception("Nejdřív vyber složku na Google Disku");
        String rootId=DocumentsContract.getTreeDocumentId(tree);
        Uri root=DocumentsContract.buildDocumentUriUsingTree(tree,rootId);
        // The selected folder itself is the app-specific cloud directory.
        Map<String,Entry> files=children(rootId);
        Entry db=files.get("projects.json");
        JSONObject local=parse(loadLocal());
        JSONObject remote=db==null?parse(""):parse(readDoc(db.uri));
        JSONObject merged=merge(local,remote);
        Uri mdir=dir(rootId,"media",true);if(mdir==null)throw new Exception("Nelze vytvořit složku media");
        syncMedia(mdir);
        if(db==null){Uri uri=DocumentsContract.createDocument(resolver,root,"application/json","projects.json");if(uri==null)throw new Exception("Nelze založit cloudovou databázi");db=new Entry(uri,"application/json");}
        writeDoc(db.uri,merged.toString());
        saveLocal(merged.toString());
        prefs.edit().putString("syncBase",nextBaseline).apply();
        return new Result("Synchronizováno · "+merged.optJSONArray("records").length()+" položek",merged);
        }
    }
}

===================== KONEC SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/SyncEngine.java =====================


==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/SyncJob.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;
import android.app.job.JobParameters;
import android.app.job.JobService;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;

/** Android periodic best-effort sync; the operating system decides exact run time. */
public class SyncJob extends JobService {
    private volatile boolean cancelled;
    @Override public boolean onStartJob(JobParameters params){
        cancelled=false;
        new Thread(()->{boolean retry=false;try{
            SharedPreferences prefs=getSharedPreferences("zamerovac",MODE_PRIVATE);
            if(!cancelled&&prefs.getString("treeUri",null)!=null)new SyncEngine(this,prefs).sync();
        }catch(Exception ex){retry=true;android.util.Log.w("Zamerovac","Scheduled sync failed",ex);}
        finally{jobFinished(params,retry&&!cancelled);}},"ScheduledSync").start();
        return true;
    }
    @Override public boolean onStopJob(JobParameters params){cancelled=true;return true;}
}

===================== KONEC SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/SyncJob.java =====================


==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/TrashPurger.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;

import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.util.AtomicFile;
import org.json.*;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.util.*;

/** Local trash only. Commit records before unlinking media; failed cleanup is durable/retryable. */
final class TrashPurger {
    // Shared with foreground saves and background sync, not just one Activity instance.
    static final Object LOCK = new Object();
    interface Writer { void write(String data) throws Exception; }
    interface Unlink { boolean delete(File file); }
    private final File home, cache;
    private final SharedPreferences prefs;
    private final Unlink unlink;
    TrashPurger(File home, File cache, SharedPreferences prefs) { this(home,cache,prefs,File::delete); }
    TrashPurger(File home, File cache, SharedPreferences prefs, Unlink unlink) {
        this.home=home;this.cache=cache;this.prefs=prefs;this.unlink=unlink;
    }
    private File pendingFile(){return new File(home,"trash-cleanup.json");}
    private static boolean validId(String id){return id.matches("[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{3,120}");}
    // Include unknown fields/history in the protection set. Shared detail photos must survive.
    private static void strings(Object value, Set<String> found) throws JSONException {
        if(value instanceof String)found.add((String)value);
        else if(value instanceof JSONObject){JSONObject o=(JSONObject)value;Iterator<String> keys=o.keys();while(keys.hasNext())strings(o.get(keys.next()),found);}
        else if(value instanceof JSONArray){JSONArray a=(JSONArray)value;for(int i=0;i<a.length();i++)strings(a.get(i),found);}
    }
    private static void media(Object value, Set<String> found) throws JSONException {
        if(value instanceof JSONObject){
            JSONObject o=(JSONObject)value;JSONArray a=o.optJSONArray("media");
            if(a!=null)for(int i=0;i<a.length();i++){String id=a.optString(i);if(validId(id))found.add(id);}
            Iterator<String> keys=o.keys();while(keys.hasNext())media(o.get(keys.next()),found);
        }else if(value instanceof JSONArray){JSONArray a=(JSONArray)value;for(int i=0;i<a.length();i++)media(a.get(i),found);}
    }
    private Set<String> pending() throws Exception {
        Set<String> ids=new TreeSet<>();AtomicFile file=new AtomicFile(pendingFile());
        if(!pendingFile().exists()&&!new File(pendingFile()+".bak").exists())return ids;
        JSONArray a=new JSONArray(new String(file.readFully(),StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        for(int i=0;i<a.length();i++){String id=a.getString(i);if(!validId(id))throw new IOException("Neplatný seznam úklidu koše");ids.add(id);}
        return ids;
    }
    private static void atomic(File target,String text) throws IOException {
        AtomicFile file=new AtomicFile(target);FileOutputStream out=null;
        try{out=file.startWrite();out.write(text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));out.getFD().sync();file.finishWrite(out);
            if(!target.isFile()||!Arrays.equals(file.readFully(),text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)))throw new IOException("Nelze ověřit zápis úklidu koše");}
        catch(IOException e){if(out!=null)file.failWrite(out);throw e;}
    }
    int pendingCount() throws Exception {return pending().size();}
    JSONObject purge(String saved,String expected,JSONArray requested,Writer writer) throws Exception {
        if(!saved.equals(expected))throw new IOException("Zakázky se mezitím změnily. Otevři koš znovu a potvrď aktuální obsah.");
        JSONObject db=new JSONObject(saved);
        if(db.optInt("schema")!=1)throw new IOException("Nepodporovaná databáze");
        JSONArray records=db.getJSONArray("records"),kept=new JSONArray();
        Set<String> ids=new HashSet<>(),candidates=pending();
        for(int i=0;i<requested.length();i++)ids.add(requested.getString(i));
        int removed=0;
        for(int i=0;i<records.length();i++){
            JSONObject r=records.getJSONObject(i);
            if(ids.contains(r.getString("id"))){
                if(!r.optBoolean("deleted"))throw new IOException("Položka již není v koši. Nic nebylo odstraněno.");
                media(r,candidates);removed++;
            }else kept.put(r);
        }
        if(removed!=ids.size())throw new IOException("Obsah koše se změnil. Potvrď jej znovu.");
        db.put("records",kept);
        // Keep only IDs, never removed payloads. Sync must not reintroduce old deleted records.
        Set<String> purged=purgedIds(db);purged.addAll(ids);
        db.put("trashPurgedIds",new JSONArray(purged));
        Set<String> protectedIds=new HashSet<>();
        JSONObject live=new JSONObject(db.toString());live.remove("trashPurgedIds");
        strings(live,protectedIds);candidates.removeAll(protectedIds);
        // Save the cleanup intent first. A failure here cannot change records or media.
        atomic(pendingFile(),new JSONArray(candidates).toString());
        String next=db.toString();
        writer.write(next); // Durable database commit. Never unlink before this succeeds.
        String warning="";
        try {
            // Recovery must not restore deleted records referring to files about to be removed.
            atomic(new File(home,"projects.bak"),next);
            Set<String> failed=new TreeSet<>();
            for(String id:candidates){
                File source=new File(home,"media/"+id),thumb=new File(cache,"photo-thumbs/"+id+".jpg");
                boolean ok=removeFile(source)&&removeFile(thumb)&&removeFile(new File(cache,"photo-thumbs/"+id+".tmp"))&&removeFile(new File(cache,"photo-thumbs/"+id+".failed"));
                if(ok){if(!prefs.edit().remove("mime-"+id).remove("name-"+id).commit())ok=false;}
                if(!ok)failed.add(id);
            }
            atomic(pendingFile(),new JSONArray(failed).toString());
            candidates=failed;
        }catch(Exception e){warning="Některé soubory se nepodařilo odstranit. Zopakuj úklid v koši.";}
        if(!candidates.isEmpty())warning="Některé soubory se nepodařilo odstranit. Zopakuj úklid v koši.";
        return new JSONObject().put("data",db).put("snapshot",next).put("pending",candidates.size()).put("warning",warning);
    }
    static Set<String> purgedIds(JSONObject db) {
        Set<String> ids=new TreeSet<>();JSONArray a=db.optJSONArray("trashPurgedIds");
        if(a!=null)for(int i=0;i<a.length();i++)ids.add(a.optString(i));
        return ids;
    }
    private boolean removeFile(File file) throws IOException {
        // No recursive delete and no links escaping the private media/cache directories.
        if(!file.exists()&&!Files.isSymbolicLink(file.toPath()))return true;
        if(Files.isSymbolicLink(file.toPath())||!file.isFile())return false;
        return unlink.delete(file);
    }
}

==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/VideoActivity.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.media.MediaPlayer;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.WindowInsets;
import android.view.WindowInsetsController;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.widget.*;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;

/** Local, seekable playback. Never rewrites or transcodes the imported original. */
public final class VideoActivity extends Activity {
    private final Handler handler=new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private VideoView video;
    private TextView status;
    private LinearLayout controls;
    private FrameLayout root;
    private Button leaveFullscreen;
    private boolean failed,paused,prepared,rendered,buffering,fullscreen,playWhenPrepared=true;
    private int position;
    private Uri uri;
    private String mime;
    private final Runnable timeout=()->failPlayback("Video se nepodařilo načíst včas.");

    /** No caller-provided path/URI and no symlink escaping the app's media directory. */
    static File localMedia(Context context,String id)throws IOException {
        if(id==null||!id.matches("[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{3,120}"))throw new IOException("Neplatný soubor");
        File directory=new File(context.getFilesDir(),"media").getCanonicalFile();
        File file=new File(directory,id).getCanonicalFile();
        if(!directory.equals(file.getParentFile())||!file.isFile())throw new IOException("Chybí soubor");
        return file;
    }
    @Override public void onCreate(Bundle saved){
        super.onCreate(saved);
        String id=getIntent().getStringExtra("mediaId");
        try{localMedia(this,id);}catch(IOException ex){finish();return;}
        uri=new Uri.Builder().scheme("content").authority(getPackageName()+".files").appendPath(id).build();
        mime=getSharedPreferences("zamerovac",0).getString("mime-"+id,"video/*");
        if(!mime.startsWith("video/"))mime="video/*"; // A chooser hint, never a metadata write.
        position=saved==null?0:Math.max(0,saved.getInt("position",0));
        playWhenPrepared=saved==null||saved.getBoolean("playing",true);
        root=new FrameLayout(this);root.setBackgroundColor(Color.BLACK);
        LinearLayout layout=new LinearLayout(this);layout.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
        root.addView(layout,new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(-1,-1));
        video=new VideoView(this){
            @Override public void start(){
                playWhenPrepared=true;
                if(!failed&&!rendered){status.setText("Načítám video…");armTimeout();}
                super.start();
            }
            @Override public void pause(){playWhenPrepared=false;super.pause();}
        };
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams videoSpace=new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(-1,0,1);videoSpace.gravity=Gravity.CENTER;
        layout.addView(video,videoSpace);
        status=new TextView(this);status.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);status.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
        status.setPadding(16,16,16,16);status.setText("Načítám video…");layout.addView(status);
        controls=new LinearLayout(this);controls.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);layout.addView(controls);
        Button enterFullscreen=new Button(this);enterFullscreen.setText("Celá obrazovka");
        enterFullscreen.setContentDescription("Přehrát video na celé obrazovce");
        enterFullscreen.setOnClickListener(v->setFullscreen(true));controls.addView(enterFullscreen);
        Button external=new Button(this);external.setText("Otevřít v jiném přehrávači");
        external.setOnClickListener(v->{
            video.pause();
            try{startActivity(Intent.createChooser(new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).setDataAndType(uri,mime)
                .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION),"Otevřít video"));}
            catch(Exception e){status.setText("Pro tento formát není dostupný jiný přehrávač.");}
        });controls.addView(external);
        Button close=new Button(this);close.setText("Zavřít video");close.setOnClickListener(v->finish());controls.addView(close);
        leaveFullscreen=new Button(this);leaveFullscreen.setText("⤢ Zpět");
        leaveFullscreen.setContentDescription("Zpět z celé obrazovky");leaveFullscreen.setMinHeight(dp(48));
        leaveFullscreen.setOnClickListener(v->setFullscreen(false));
        root.addView(leaveFullscreen,new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(-2,-2,Gravity.TOP|Gravity.RIGHT));
        root.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener((view,insets)->{
            int left,top,right,bottom;
            if(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=30){
                android.graphics.Insets bars=insets.getInsetsIgnoringVisibility(WindowInsets.Type.systemBars()|WindowInsets.Type.displayCutout());
                left=bars.left;top=bars.top;right=bars.right;bottom=bars.bottom;
            }else{left=insets.getStableInsetLeft();top=insets.getStableInsetTop();right=insets.getStableInsetRight();bottom=insets.getStableInsetBottom();}
            root.setPadding(fullscreen?0:left,fullscreen?0:top,fullscreen?0:right,fullscreen?0:bottom);
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams exit=(FrameLayout.LayoutParams)leaveFullscreen.getLayoutParams();
            exit.topMargin=top+dp(8);exit.rightMargin=right+dp(8);leaveFullscreen.setLayoutParams(exit);
            return insets;
        });
        setContentView(root);
        if(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=33)getOnBackInvokedDispatcher().registerOnBackInvokedCallback(
            android.window.OnBackInvokedDispatcher.PRIORITY_DEFAULT,this::handleBack);
        setFullscreen(saved!=null&&saved.getBoolean("fullscreen",false));
        MediaController playback=new MediaController(this);playback.setAnchorView(video);video.setMediaController(playback);
        video.setOnErrorListener((player,what,extra)->{failPlayback("Video nelze přehrát. Formát nebo kodek nemusí být podporován.");return true;});
        video.setOnPreparedListener(player->{
            if(failed)return;
            prepared=true;handler.removeCallbacks(timeout);status.setText("Video připraveno.");
            if(position>0)video.seekTo(position);
            if(!paused&&playWhenPrepared)video.start();
        });
        video.setOnCompletionListener(player->{if(!failed){playWhenPrepared=false;handler.removeCallbacks(timeout);status.setText("Video skončilo.");}});
        video.setOnInfoListener((player,what,extra)->{
            if(failed)return true;
            if(what==MediaPlayer.MEDIA_INFO_BUFFERING_START){if(!buffering)armTimeout();buffering=true;status.setText("Načítám video…");}
            if(what==MediaPlayer.MEDIA_INFO_BUFFERING_END||what==MediaPlayer.MEDIA_INFO_VIDEO_RENDERING_START){
                if(what==MediaPlayer.MEDIA_INFO_VIDEO_RENDERING_START)rendered=true;
                buffering=false;handler.removeCallbacks(timeout);status.setText("");
            }
            return false;
        });
        armTimeout();video.setVideoURI(uri);
    }
    private int dp(int value){return Math.round(value*getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);}
    private void setFullscreen(boolean enabled){
        fullscreen=enabled;controls.setVisibility(enabled?View.GONE:View.VISIBLE);
        status.setVisibility(enabled&&!failed?View.GONE:View.VISIBLE);
        leaveFullscreen.setVisibility(enabled?View.VISIBLE:View.GONE);
        applySystemBars();root.requestApplyInsets(); // Reuse the same VideoView and decoder.
    }
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation") private void applySystemBars(){
        int layout=View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_STABLE|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_FULLSCREEN|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_HIDE_NAVIGATION;
        if(fullscreen)getWindow().addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_FULLSCREEN);
        else getWindow().clearFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_FULLSCREEN);
        if(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=30){
            getWindow().setDecorFitsSystemWindows(false);
            WindowInsetsController bars=getWindow().getInsetsController();
            if(bars!=null){
                bars.setSystemBarsBehavior(WindowInsetsController.BEHAVIOR_SHOW_TRANSIENT_BARS_BY_SWIPE);
                if(fullscreen)bars.hide(WindowInsets.Type.systemBars());else bars.show(WindowInsets.Type.systemBars());
            }
        }else getWindow().getDecorView().setSystemUiVisibility(layout|(fullscreen?
            View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_FULLSCREEN|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_HIDE_NAVIGATION|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_IMMERSIVE_STICKY:0));
    }
    private void handleBack(){if(fullscreen)setFullscreen(false);else finish();}
    @Override @SuppressWarnings("deprecation") public void onBackPressed(){handleBack();}
    @Override public void onWindowFocusChanged(boolean focused){super.onWindowFocusChanged(focused);if(focused&&root!=null)applySystemBars();}
    private void armTimeout(){handler.removeCallbacks(timeout);if(!failed&&!paused)handler.postDelayed(timeout,20000);}
    private void failPlayback(String message){
        if(failed)return;failed=true;handler.removeCallbacks(timeout);
        if(video!=null)video.stopPlayback();if(status!=null){status.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);status.setText(message);}
    }
    @Override protected void onPause(){
        paused=true;if(video!=null){
            if(prepared)position=video.getCurrentPosition();
            boolean playing=playWhenPrepared;video.pause();playWhenPrepared=playing;
        }
        handler.removeCallbacks(timeout);super.onPause();
    }
    @Override protected void onResume(){super.onResume();paused=false;if(video!=null&&!failed&&(!prepared||buffering))armTimeout();}
    @Override protected void onSaveInstanceState(Bundle saved){
        saved.putInt("position",video==null||!prepared||paused?position:video.getCurrentPosition());
        saved.putBoolean("fullscreen",fullscreen);saved.putBoolean("playing",playWhenPrepared);super.onSaveInstanceState(saved);
    }
    @Override protected void onDestroy(){handler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);if(video!=null)video.stopPlayback();super.onDestroy();}
}

==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/VideoThumbnails.java ====================
package cz.zamerovac.app;

import android.graphics.*;
import android.media.MediaMetadataRetriever;
import android.os.Build;
import java.io.*;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

/** Bounded background decoding. A broken codec cannot hold the database/purge lock. */
final class VideoThumbnails implements AutoCloseable {
    private final java.util.function.Supplier<MediaMetadataRetriever> decoder;
    private final long waitMillis;
    VideoThumbnails(){this(MediaMetadataRetriever::new,5000);}
    VideoThumbnails(java.util.function.Supplier<MediaMetadataRetriever> decoder,long waitMillis){this.decoder=decoder;this.waitMillis=waitMillis;}
    private final ThreadPoolExecutor workers=new ThreadPoolExecutor(1,2,30,TimeUnit.SECONDS,
        new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(4),task->{Thread thread=new Thread(task,"VideoThumbnail");thread.setDaemon(true);return thread;});
    InputStream open(File source,File directory)throws IOException {
        if(!source.isFile())return null;
        File cached=new File(directory,source.getName()+".jpg"),failed=new File(directory,source.getName()+".failed");
        if(cached.isFile()&&cached.lastModified()>=source.lastModified())return new FileInputStream(cached);
        if(failed.isFile()&&failed.lastModified()>=source.lastModified())return null;
        try{
            Future<File> job=workers.submit(()->generate(source,directory,cached,failed));
            File ready=job.get(waitMillis,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
            return ready==null?null:new FileInputStream(ready);
        }catch(InterruptedException ex){Thread.currentThread().interrupt();return null;}
        catch(ExecutionException|TimeoutException|RejectedExecutionException ex){return null;}
    }
    private File generate(File source,File directory,File cached,File failed)throws IOException {
        if(!directory.isDirectory()&&!directory.mkdirs()&&!directory.isDirectory())return null;
        long modified=source.lastModified();
        MediaMetadataRetriever retriever=decoder.get();Bitmap frame=null,thumb=null;
        File temporary=new File(directory,source.getName()+"-"+UUID.randomUUID()+".tmp");
        boolean success=false,unsupported=false;
        try{
            retriever.setDataSource(source.getAbsolutePath());
            if(Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=27)frame=retriever.getScaledFrameAtTime(0,MediaMetadataRetriever.OPTION_CLOSEST_SYNC,288,288);
            else{
                // API 26 cannot sample a frame. Decline huge/unknown dimensions safely.
                int w=Integer.parseInt(retriever.extractMetadata(MediaMetadataRetriever.METADATA_KEY_VIDEO_WIDTH));
                int h=Integer.parseInt(retriever.extractMetadata(MediaMetadataRetriever.METADATA_KEY_VIDEO_HEIGHT));
                if(w<=0||h<=0||(long)w*h>4000000){unsupported=true;return null;}
                frame=retriever.getFrameAtTime(0,MediaMetadataRetriever.OPTION_CLOSEST_SYNC);
            }
            if(frame==null){unsupported=true;return null;}
            thumb=Bitmap.createBitmap(144,144,Bitmap.Config.RGB_565);
            float side=Math.min(frame.getWidth(),frame.getHeight());
            Rect crop=new Rect((int)((frame.getWidth()-side)/2),(int)((frame.getHeight()-side)/2),
                (int)((frame.getWidth()+side)/2),(int)((frame.getHeight()+side)/2));
            new Canvas(thumb).drawBitmap(frame,crop,new RectF(0,0,144,144),new Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG|Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG));
            try(OutputStream out=new FileOutputStream(temporary)){if(!thumb.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG,82,out))return null;}
            synchronized(TrashPurger.LOCK){
                if(Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()||!source.isFile()||source.lastModified()!=modified)return null;
                java.nio.file.Files.move(temporary.toPath(),cached.toPath(),java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
                cached.setLastModified(modified);failed.delete();success=true;return cached;
            }
        }catch(RuntimeException ex){unsupported=true;return null;}
        finally{
            if(thumb!=null)thumb.recycle();if(frame!=null)frame.recycle();
            try{retriever.release();}catch(Exception ignored){}
            temporary.delete();
            if(!success&&unsupported&&!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted())synchronized(TrashPurger.LOCK){
                if(source.isFile()&&source.lastModified()==modified){
                    try{failed.createNewFile();failed.setLastModified(modified);}catch(IOException ignored){}
                }
            }
        }
    }
    @Override public void close(){workers.shutdownNow();}
}
