==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: tests/test_imagemeter_inset.py ====================
"""Reference checks for inset-detail-photo handling (ImageMeter comparison).

Does not change touch targets or stored dimensions of older notes.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import unittest

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
NOTES=(ROOT/'app/src/main/assets/rich-notes.js').read_text('utf-8')
INSET=(ROOT/'app/src/main/assets/inset-workspace.js').read_text('utf-8')


class ImageMeterInsetTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_new_inset_is_large_enough_and_existing_size_survives(self):
        self.assertIn('Number(item.insetWidth)||Math.max(140,Math.min(1400,Math.round(photoSize.w*.42)))', NOTES)
        self.assertIn("item.insetWidth=Math.max(70,Math.min(1500", NOTES)

    def test_original_image_remains_editable(self):
        self.assertIn('function openNestedPhoto(parent,item,mediaId)', NOTES)
        self.assertIn("item.detailItems||[]", NOTES)
        self.assertIn('window.renderPhotoInset=function', INSET)

    def test_image_border_stays_yellow_when_selected(self):
        self.assertIn("c.strokeStyle='#f7dc27';c.lineWidth=isSelected?2.4:2", INSET)
        self.assertNotIn("isSelected?'#e3c4ff'", INSET)

    def test_grips_match_video_without_changing_hit_targets(self):
        self.assertIn("c.arc(g.cx,g.cy,11", INSET)
        self.assertIn("rgba(255,255,255,.46)", INSET)
        self.assertIn("rgba(255,255,255,.72)", INSET)
        self.assertIn("rotationHandle(c,turn(", INSET)
        self.assertIn("const near=position=>pointerDistance(point,position)<=24", INSET)
        self.assertIn("kind:'resize-right'", INSET)
        self.assertIn("kind:'resize-left'", INSET)

    def test_detail_can_leave_photo_without_moving_note_anchor(self):
        self.assertIn("v.item.insetX=v.startingX+dx/view.zoom", INSET)
        self.assertIn("v.item.insetY=v.startingY+dy/view.zoom", INSET)
        self.assertIn("v.item.a=boundsPoint(w)", INSET)
        self.assertIn("v.item.insetMoved=true", INSET)


if __name__=='__main__':
    unittest.main()

==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: tests/test_project.py ====================
"""Sanity checks; the project must additionally build against the Android SDK."""
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import unittest
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ASSETS = ROOT/'app/src/main/assets'
class SourceSmokeTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_javascript_syntax(self):
        for name in ('app.js','editor.js'):
            subprocess.run(['node','--check',str(ASSETS/name)],check=True,capture_output=True)
    def test_manifest_and_resources(self):
        for path in (ROOT/'app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml',ROOT/'app/src/main/res/values/styles.xml',ROOT/'app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher.xml'):
            ET.parse(path)
    def test_editor_controls_present(self):
        scripts=(ASSETS/'app.js').read_text()+(ASSETS/'editor.js').read_text()
        for item in ('drawLoupe','checkpoint','commitEditor','onNativeMedia','onNativeSync','onLaserValue','syncCloud','openNote'):
            self.assertIn(item,scripts)
    def test_excluded_tools(self):
        tools=(ASSETS/'editor.js').read_text()
        for forbidden in ('Počítadlo','Výsledková listina','Perspektivní délka','Referenční měřítko'):
            self.assertNotIn(forbidden,tools)
    def test_dimension_endpoints_match_grips_and_loupe(self):
        # Regression: the previous -22px visual offset put the dimension's
        # visible endpoint at a different location than its editable anchor.
        # The magnifier correctly centered the anchor, not the visible arrow.
        editor=(ASSETS/'editor.js').read_text()
        # Geometrie, popisek i bílé zvýraznění používají stejný posun.
        self.assertEqual(editor.count('item.offset??0'), 3)
        self.assertNotIn('item.offset??-22', editor)
        self.assertIn('worldToScreen(item[key])', editor)
        self.assertIn('magnifierPoint=selectedHandle.point', editor)

    def test_photo_waits_before_drawing_annotations(self):
        editor=(ASSETS/'editor.js').read_text('utf-8')
        self.assertIn("if(current.kind==='photo'&&!stageImg)",editor)
        self.assertIn("photoLoadFailed?'Fotografii nelze načíst. Zavři ji a otevři znovu.':'Načítám fotografii…'",editor)
        self.assertIn("if(!active())return;clearTimeout(photoLoadTimer);photoLoadFailed=false;stageImg=img",editor)
        self.assertIn("generation===photoLoadGeneration&&current===r&&screen==='editor'",editor)

    def test_reference_magnifier_geometry(self):
        editor=(ASSETS/'editor.js').read_text()
        css=(ASSETS/'style.css').read_text()
        # Comparison screenshots: ~160 actual pixels on phone = 136 CSS px.
        self.assertIn('const size=136;', editor)
        self.assertIn('border-radius:32px', css)
        self.assertIn('loupeCtx.setLineDash([5,7])', editor)
        self.assertIn('size*.5-pointer.x*zoom,size*.5-pointer.y*zoom', editor)

    def test_installed_version_visible_to_user(self):
        activity=(ROOT/'app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/MainActivity.java').read_text()
        app=(ASSETS/'app.js').read_text()
        css=(ASSETS/'style.css').read_text()
        self.assertIn('getPackageManager().getPackageInfo(getPackageName(),0).versionName', activity)
        self.assertIn('Native.getAppVersion()', app)
        self.assertIn('id="appVersionInfo"', app)
        self.assertIn('#screenTitle .app-version', css)

    def test_photo_thumbnails_and_menu(self):
        app=(ASSETS/'app.js').read_text()
        css=(ASSETS/'style.css').read_text()
        activity=(ROOT/'app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/MainActivity.java').read_text()
        self.assertIn('function browserEntryIcon(r)', app)
        self.assertIn('entry-thumb', app)
        self.assertIn('photoThumbnail(id)', activity)
        self.assertIn('"/thumb/"', activity)
        self.assertIn('.entry-icon.photo-preview', css)
        # "Zakázky" was a no-op that only called showFolder on itself.
        self.assertNotIn("['📁  Zakázky',()=>showFolder()]", app)

    def test_photo_capture_time_and_readable_location(self):
        app=(ASSETS/'app.js').read_text()
        activity=(ROOT/'app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/MainActivity.java').read_text()
        resolver=(ROOT/'app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app/PhotoAddressResolver.java').read_text()
        manifest=(ROOT/'app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml').read_text()
        self.assertIn('java.time.Instant.now().toString()', activity)
        self.assertIn('ExifInterface.TAG_DATETIME_ORIGINAL', activity)
        self.assertIn('PhotoAddressResolver.resolve', activity)
        self.assertIn('window.onNativePhotoAddress=', app)
        self.assertIn('photoTakenAt', app)
        self.assertIn('photoAddress', app)
        self.assertIn('getFromLocation(', resolver)
        self.assertIn('ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION', manifest)
        self.assertIn('ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION', manifest)
        self.assertNotIn('latitude:', app)
        self.assertNotIn('longitude:', app)

    def test_cloud_and_media_are_real_modules(self):
        java=ROOT/'app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app'
        for module in ('SyncEngine.java','SyncJob.java','BoschClassicReader.java','MediaProvider.java'):
            self.assertTrue((java/module).is_file())
if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main()

==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: tests/test_upgrade.py ====================
"""Tests průchodnosti velké aktualizace a zásad ochrany stávajících dat."""
from pathlib import Path
import unittest
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ASSETS = ROOT / 'app/src/main/assets'
JAVA = ROOT / 'app/src/main/java/cz/zamerovac/app'


class BigUpdateSmokeTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_added_javascript_compiles(self):
        for name in ('upgrade.js', 'rich-notes.js', 'sketch.js'):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertTrue((ASSETS / name).is_file())
                subprocess.run(['node', '--check', str(ASSETS / name)],
                               check=True, capture_output=True)

    def test_new_modules_are_loaded_from_app_index(self):
        html = (ASSETS / 'index.html').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        for file in ('upgrade.css', 'upgrade.js', 'rich-notes.js', 'sketch.js'):
            self.assertIn(file, html)
        self.assertLess(html.index('editor.js'), html.index('upgrade.js'))
        self.assertLess(html.index('upgrade.js'), html.index('rich-notes.js'))

    def test_deferred_ideas_are_not_claimed_as_shipped(self):
        upgrade = (ASSETS / 'upgrade.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        self.assertNotIn('floorPlanCameraMarkers', upgrade)
        self.assertNotIn('automaticSamsungNotesSync', upgrade)

    def test_preview_id_and_signing_are_not_changed(self):
        gradle = (ROOT / 'app/build.gradle').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        self.assertIn("applicationId 'cz.zamerovac.app'", gradle)
        self.assertIn("applicationIdSuffix '.preview'", gradle)
        self.assertIn("keyAlias 'zamerovacdev'", gradle)
        self.assertIn("manifestPlaceholders = [appLabel: 'Zaměřovač']", gradle)
        self.assertIn('versionCode 1000 + workflowBuildNumber', gradle)

    def test_backup_includes_originals_and_isolated_restore(self):
        main = (JAVA / 'MainActivity.java').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        restore = (JAVA / 'BackupImporter.java').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        self.assertIn('new ZipEntry("projects.json")', main)
        self.assertIn('new ZipEntry("media-meta.json")', main)
        self.assertIn('new ZipEntry("media/"+id)', main)
        self.assertIn('UUID.randomUUID()', restore)
        self.assertIn('restoredTitle(records)', restore)
        self.assertIn('MAX_ENTRIES', restore)
        self.assertIn('collectNestedMedia', main)
        self.assertIn('rewriteItems', restore)
        self.assertIn('writeDb(restored.data.toString())', main)

    def test_photo_notes_contain_editable_detail(self):
        editor = (ASSETS / 'editor.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        rich = (ASSETS / 'rich-notes.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        # Nová poznámka vzniká okamžitě v editoru i bez textu.
        self.assertIn("else if(mode==='note'){", editor)
        self.assertIn("item.captionMoved=true", editor)
        self.assertNotIn("else if(mode==='note'){openRichNote(item,true)}", editor)
        self.assertIn("openRichNote(item,false)", editor)
        self.assertIn('detailItems', rich)
        self.assertIn('openNestedPhoto', rich)
        self.assertIn('Native.startAudio', rich)

    def test_diagnostics_and_version_history_present(self):
        upgrade = (ASSETS / 'upgrade.js').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        for name in ('openDiagnostics', 'showChanges', 'openSumCheck',
                     'restoreVersion', 'setCloudState', 'onAddressCandidates',
                     'isRecordLocked', 'showLaserHistory', 'navigateToFeature'):
            self.assertIn(name, upgrade)
        self.assertIn('window.onLaserStatus', upgrade)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

==================== ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: tests/trash_ui_interaction.js ====================
/* Run the real native-result UI path: active editor objects must keep their identity. */
const assert=require('node:assert/strict'),fs=require('node:fs'),vm=require('node:vm');
const src=fs.readFileSync('app/src/main/assets/app.js','utf8');
const code=src.slice(src.indexOf('let trashBusy=false;'),src.indexOf('function confirmTrashPurge('));
const active={id:'active',items:[{measure:'100,000000123',unit:'in'}]},note={id:'note',text:'rozepsaná poznámka'},gone={id:'gone',deleted:true};
const elements={okModal:{disabled:false}},messages=[];
let calls=0,folders=0,trash=0;
const env={native:true,dataLoadFailed:false,persistedSnapshot:'last-disk-snapshot',state:{schema:1,records:[active,note,gone]},prefs:{},screen:'editor',
 Native:{purgeTrash(expected,desired,ids){calls++;assert.equal(expected,'last-disk-snapshot');assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(ids),['gone']);const data=JSON.parse(desired);data.records=data.records.filter(r=>!r.deleted);return JSON.stringify({data,snapshot:JSON.stringify(data),pending:0})}},
 $:id=>elements[id],notify:m=>messages.push(m),showFolder:()=>folders++,showTrash:()=>trash++};
vm.createContext(env);vm.runInContext(code,env);
env.purgeTrashRecords(['gone']);
assert.equal(calls,1);assert.equal(folders,0);assert.equal(trash,1);
assert.equal(env.state.records[0],active);assert.equal(env.state.records[1],note);
active.items.push({measure:'999,000123',unit:'mm'});note.text+=' pokračování';
assert.equal(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(env.state)).records[0].items.length,2);
assert.equal(env.state.records[1].text,'rozepsaná poznámka pokračování');
assert.equal(elements.okModal.disabled,false);
// Native failure must retain the whole current state and the last known disk snapshot.
const before=env.state,snapshot=env.persistedSnapshot;
env.Native.purgeTrash=()=>JSON.stringify({error:'Concurrent database change'});
env.purgeTrashRecords([]);assert.equal(env.state,before);assert.equal(env.persistedSnapshot,snapshot);
assert(messages.at(-1).includes('Concurrent database change'));
// Simulated reentrant tap cannot start a second purge while the first native call is pending.
env.Native.purgeTrash=()=>{env.purgeTrashRecords([]);return JSON.stringify({error:'failed once'})};
env.purgeTrashRecords([]);assert.equal(elements.okModal.disabled,false);
console.log('OK: native purge preserves live editor/note references, exact values, stale-snapshot errors and repeated taps');

===================== KONEC SOUBORU: tests/trash_ui_interaction.js =====================
